Returns true if the process\n357\t    /// exists (including EPERM — process exists but we can't signal it).\n358\t    pub fn check_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n359\t        if pid == 0 {\n360\t            return false;\n361\t        }\n362\t        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence; no signal is delivered.\n363\t        let rc = unsafe { libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t, 0) };\n364\t        if rc == 0 {\n365\t            return true;\n366\t        }\n367\t        // ESRCH means no such process. hb in heartbeats {\n381\t            // Skip our own heartbeat.\n382\t            if hb.qualified_id == self.qualified_id {\n383\t                continue;\n384\t            }\n385\t\n386\t            if Self::is_stale(&hb, self.heartbeat_ttl) {\n387\t                let pid_alive = Self::check_pid_alive(hb.pid);\n388\t                let hb_file = hb.heartbeat_file.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n389\t                    self.heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", hb.qualified_id))\n390\t                });\n391\t                stale.push(StalePeer {\n392\t                    worker_id: hb.worker_id.clone(),\n393\t                    qualified_id: Some(hb.qualified_id.clone()),\n394\t                    pid: hb.pid,\n395\t                    pid_alive,\n396\t                    current_bead: hb.current_bead.clone(),\n397\t                    last_heartbeat: hb.last_heartbeat,\n398\t                    heartbeat_file: hb_file,\n399\t                });\n400\t            }\n401\t        }\n402\t\n403\t        Ok(stale)\n404\t    }\n405\t\n406\t    // ── Internal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n407\t\n408\t    /// Write a heartbeat file atomically (write temp, then rename).\n409\t    fn write_heartbeat(&self) -> Result<()> {\n410\t        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) = {\n411\t            let guard = self\n412\t                .shared_state\n413\t                .lock()\n414\t                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"shared state lock poisoned: {e}\"))?;\n415\t            (\n416\t                guard.state.clone(),\n417\t                guard.current_bead.clone(),\n418\t                guard.beads_processed,\n419\t                guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n420\t                guard.model.clone(),\n421\t            )\n422\t        };\n423\t\n424\t        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n425\t        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| self.workspace.clone());\n426\t\n427\t        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n428\t        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n429\t\n430\t        let data = HeartbeatData {\n431\t            worker_id: self.worker_id.clone(),\n432\t            qualified_id: self.qualified_id.clone(),\n433\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n434\t            state,\n435\t            current_bead,\n436\t            workspace: effective_workspace,\n437\t            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n438\t            started_at: self.started_at,\n439\t            beads_processed,\n440\t            session: self.worker_id.clone(),\n441\t            is_idle,\n442\t            current_task,\n443\t            model,\n444\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n445\t        };\n446\t\n447\t        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n448\t        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n449\t\n450\t        // Auto-create parent directory so that heartbeats self-recover if the\n451\t        // directory is deleted while a worker is running.\n452\t        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n453\t            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)\n454\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create heartbeat dir: {}\", parent.display()))?;\n455\t        }\n456\t\n457\t        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data).context(\"failed to serialize heartbeat\")?;\n458\t        std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes()).with_context(|| {\n459\t            format!(\n460\t                \"failed to write temp heartbeat file: {}\",\n461\t                tmp_path.display()\n462\t            )\n463\t        })?;\n464\t        std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path).with_context(|| {\n465\t            format!(\n466\t                \"failed to rename heartbeat file: {} -> {}\",\n467\t                tmp_path.display(),\n468\t                path.display()\n469\t            )\n470\t        })?;\n471\t\n472\t        Ok(())\n473\t    }\n474\t}\n475\t\n476\timpl Drop for HealthMonitor {\n477\t    fn drop(&mut self) {\n478\t        // Best-effort: signal the emitter and clean up the heartbeat file.\n479\t        self.stop();\n480\t    }\n481\t}\n482\t\n483\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n484\t// StalePeer\n485\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n486\t\n487\t/// A peer worker detected as having a stale heartbeat.\n488\t#[derive(Debug)]\n489\tpub struct StalePeer {\n490\t    pub worker_id: String,\n491\t    /// Fully-qualified identity of the peer.\n492\t    pub qualified_id: Option<String>,\n493\t    pub pid: u32,\n494\t    pub pid_alive: bool,\n495\t    pub current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n496\t    pub last_heartbeat: DateTime<Utc>,\n497\t    pub heartbeat_file: PathBuf,\n498\t}\n499\t\n500\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n501\t// Emitter loop (runs in a dedicated std::thread)\n502\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n503\t\n504\t/// Maximum sleep between heartbeat attempts when backing off on failure.\n505\tconst MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5 * 60);\n506\t\n507\t/// Sleep interval for interruptible sleep — check shutdown flag every 100ms.\n508\t///\n509\t/// This ensures the heartbeat emitter responds to shutdown signals within 100ms\n510\t/// instead of sleeping for the full heartbeat interval (default 60s). When the\n511\t/// worker is killed (e.g., by the capacity governor), this gives the atexit\n512\t/// handler a better chance to emit worker.stopped telemetry.\n513\tconst INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);\n514\t\n515\t/// Background emitter loop. Writes heartbeat at each interval.\n516\t///\n517\t/// Circuit breaker: after `max_consecutive_failures` consecutive write failures\n518\t/// the loop sets the shutdown flag and exits so the worker terminates instead of\n519\t/// spinning indefinitely.\n520\t///\n521\t/// Backoff: each consecutive failure doubles the inter-attempt sleep, capped at\n522\t/// [`MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF`].\n523\t///\n524\t/// Uses an interruptible sleep pattern to respond quickly to shutdown signals.\n525\t#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n526\tfn emitter_loop(\n527\t    shared_state: Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>,\n528\t    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n529\t    heartbeat_dir: PathBuf,\n530\t    worker_id: String,\n531\t    qualified_id: String,\n532\t    workspace: PathBuf,\n533\t    started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n534\t    interval: Duration,\n535\t    max_consecutive_failures: u32,\n536\t) {\n537\t    // Ensure the heartbeat directory exists before entering the write loop so\n538\t    // that workers self-recover if ~/.needle/state/heartbeats/ is deleted.\n539\t    if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&heartbeat_dir) {\n540\t        tracing::error!(\n541\t            error = %e,\n542\t            dir = %heartbeat_dir.display(),\n543\t            \"failed to create heartbeat directory\"\n544\t        );\n545\t    }\n546\t\n547\t    let mut consecutive_failures: u32 = 0;\n548\t    let mut current_sleep = interval;\n549\t    let mut elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n550\t\n551\t    loop {\n552\t        // Interruptible sleep: check shutdown flag every 100ms instead of\n553\t        // sleeping for the full interval. This ensures the emitter responds\n554\t        // to shutdown signals quickly, giving the atexit handler a chance to\n555\t        // emit worker.stopped telemetry even if the process is killed.\n556\t        while elapsed < current_sleep {\n557\t            if shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n558\t                tracing::debug!(worker = %worker_id, \"heartbeat emitter shutting down\");\n559\t                return;\n560\t            }\n561\t            let sleep_dur = std::cmp::min(INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL, current_sleep - elapsed);\n562\t            std::thread::sleep(sleep_dur);\n563\t            elapsed += sleep_dur;\n564\t        }\n565\t        elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n566\t\n567\t        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) =\n568\t            match shared_state.lock() {\n569\t                Ok(guard) => (\n570\t                    guard.state.clone(),\n571\t                    guard.current_bead.clone(),\n572\t                    guard.beads_processed,\n573\t                    guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n574\t                    guard.model.clone(),\n575\t                ),\n576\t                Err(_) => {\n577\t                    // Mutex poisoned — the main thread panicked. Exit.\n578\t                    tracing::error!(\n579\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n580\t                        \"shared state mutex poisoned, heartbeat emitter exiting\"\n581\t                    );\n582\t                    return;\n583\t                }\n584\t            };\n585\t\n586\t        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n587\t        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| workspace.clone());\n588\t\n589\t        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n590\t        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n591\t\n592\t        let data = HeartbeatData {\n593\t            worker_id: worker_id.clone(),\n594\t            qualified_id: qualified_id.clone(),\n595\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n596\t            state,\n597\t            current_bead,\n598\t            workspace: effective_workspace,\n599\t            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n600\t            started_at,\n601\t            beads_processed,\n602\t            session: worker_id.clone(),\n603\t            is_idle,\n604\t            current_task,\n605\t            model,\n606\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n607\t        };\n608\t\n609\t        let path = heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", qualified_id));\n610\t        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n611\t\n612\t        let write_result: anyhow::Result<()> = (|| {\n613\t            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data)?;\n614\t            std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes())?;\n615\t            std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path)?;\n616\t            Ok(())\n617\t        })();\n618\t\n619\t        match write_result {\n620\t            Ok(()) => {\n621\t                consecutive_failures = 0;\n622\t                current_sleep = interval;\n623\t            }\n624\t            Err(e) => {\n625\t                consecutive_failures += 1;\n626\t                tracing::error!(\n627\t                    error = %e,\n628\t                    worker = %worker_id,\n629\t                    consecutive_failures,\n630\t                    max = max_consecutive_failures,\n631\t                    \"heartbeat write failed\"\n632\t                );\n633\t                if consecutive_failures >= max_consecutive_failures {\n634\t                    tracing::error!(\n635\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n636\t                        consecutive_failures,\n637\t                        \"heartbeat emitter circuit breaker triggered — worker will shut down\"\n638\t                    );\n639\t                    // Emit a final heartbeat event before shutting down so the\n640\t                    // telemetry log shows the circuit breaker was the cause.\n641\t                    let _ = std::fs::write(\n642\t                        heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}-circuit-breaker.txt\", qualified_id)),\n643\t                        format!(\n644\t                            \"Circuit breaker tripped after {} consecutive heartbeat write failures\\n\\\n645\t                             Worker: {}\\n\\\n646\t                             Qualified ID: {}\\n\\\n647\t                             Last error: {}\\n\\\n648\t                             Timestamp: {}\",\n649\t                            consecutive_failures,\n650\t                            worker_id,\n651\t                            qualified_id,\n652\t                            e,\n653\t                            Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n654\t                        ),\n655\t                    );\n656\t                    shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n657\t                    return;\n658\t                }\n659\t                // Exponential backoff to reduce log spam before the circuit breaker fires.\n660\t                current_sleep = current_sleep.saturating_mul(2).min(MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF);\n661\t            }\n662\t        }\n663\t    }\n664\t}\n665\t\n666\t// false\n67\t    }\n68\t}\n69\t\n70\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n71\t// WorkerEntry\n72\t// );\n202\t\n203\t        Ok(())\n204\t    }\n205\t\n206\t    /// Update the worker state visible to the heartbeat emitter.\n207\t    ///\n208\t    /// Called by the worker on every state transition.\n209\t    pub fn update_state(\n210\t        &self,\n211\t        state: &WorkerState,\n212\t        current_bead: Option<&BeadId>,\n213\t        workspace: Option<&Path>,\n214\t    ) {\n215\t        if let Ok(mut guard) = self.shared_state.lock() {\n216\t            guard.state = state.clone();\n217\t            guard.current_bead = current_bead.cloned();\n218\t            guard.current_workspace = workspace.map(|p| p.to_path_buf());\n219\t        }\n220\t    }\n221\t\n222\t    /// Update the beads_processed count visible to the heartbeat emitter.\n223\t    pub fn update_beads_processed(&self, count: u64) {\n224\t        if let Ok(mut guard) = self.shared_state.lock() {\n225\t            guard.beads_processed = count;\n226\t        }\n227\t    }\n228\t\n229\t    /// Stop the heartbeat emitter and remove this worker's heartbeat file.\n230\t    ///\n231\t    /// Called on graceful shutdown (STOPPED) and best-effort on ERRORED.\n232\t    pub fn stop(&mut self) {\n233\t        // Signal the emitter thread to exit.\n234\t        self.shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n235\t\n236\t        // Join the emitter thread (with a timeout to avoid hanging).\n237\t        if let Some(handle) = self.emitter_handle.take() {\n238\t            // Give the thread up to 2x the interval to notice shutdown and exit.\n239\t            let _ = handle.join();\n240\t        }\n241\t\n242\t        // Remove the heartbeat file (best-effort).\n243\t        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n244\t        if path.exists() {\n245\t            if let Err(e) = std::fs::remove_file(&path) {\n246\t                tracing::warn!(\n247\t                    error = %e,\n248\t                    path = %path.display(),\n249\t                    \"failed to remove heartbeat file on shutdown\"\n250\t                );\n251\t            } else {\n252\t                tracing::debug!(path = %path.display(), \"heartbeat file removed\");\n253\t            }\n254\t        }\n255\t    }\n256\t\n257\t    /// Path to this worker's heartbeat file.\n258\t    ///\n259\t    /// Keyed by fully-qualified identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`) to prevent\n260\t    /// collisions when workers from different adapter pools share a NATO name.\n261\t    pub fn heartbeat_path(&self) -> PathBuf {\n262\t        self.heartbeat_dir\n263\t            .join(format!(\"{}.json\", self.qualified_id))\n264\t    }\n265\t\n266\t    /// The fully-qualified identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`).\n267\t    pub fn qualified_id(&self) -> &str {\n268\t        &self.qualified_id\n269\t    }\n270\t\n271\t    /// Directory where heartbeat files are stored.\n272\t    pub fn heartbeat_dir(&self) -> &Path {\n273\t        &self.heartbeat_dir\n274\t    }\n275\t\n276\t    /// The configured heartbeat TTL.\n277\t    pub fn heartbeat_ttl(&self) -> Duration {\n278\t        self.heartbeat_ttl\n279\t    }\n280\t\n281\t    // ── Reader utilities (used by peer monitoring / Mend strand) ────────────\n282\t\n283\t    /// Read all heartbeat files in the given directory.\n284\t    ///\n285\t    /// Silently skips files that cannot be read or parsed (they may be\n286\t    /// partially written or from a crashed worker).\n287\t    pub fn read_all_heartbeats(dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<HeartbeatData>> {\n288\t        let mut heartbeats = Vec::new();\n289\t\n290\t        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {\n291\t            Ok(e) => e,\n292\t            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(heartbeats),\n293\t            Err(e) => {\n294\t                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n295\t                    \"failed to read heartbeat directory {}: {}\",\n296\t                    dir.display(),\n297\t                    e\n298\t                ));\n299\t            }\n300\t        };\n301\t\n302\t        for entry in entries {\n303\t            let entry = match entry {\n304\t                Ok(e) => e,\n305\t                Err(_) => continue,\n306\t            };\n307\t\n308\t            let path = entry.path();\n309\t            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some(\"json\") {\n310\t                continue;\n311\t            }\n312\t\n313\t            match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n314\t                Ok(content) => match serde_json::from_str::<HeartbeatData>(&content) {\n315\t                    Ok(mut hb) => {\n316\t                        // Backfill qualified_id for heartbeats written by older versions.\n317\t                        if hb.qualified_id.is_empty() {\n318\t                            hb.qualified_id = hb.worker_id.clone();\n319\t                        }\n320\t                        hb.heartbeat_file = Some(path.clone());\n321\t                        heartbeats.push(hb)\n322\t                    }\n323\t                    Err(e) => {\n324\t                        tracing::debug!(\n325\t                            path = %path.display(),\n326\t                            error = %e,\n327\t                            \"skipping unparseable heartbeat file\"\n328\t                        );\n329\t                    }\n330\t                },\n331\t                Err(e) => {\n332\t                    tracing::debug!(\n333\t                        path = %path.display(),\n334\t                        error = %e,\n335\t                        \"skipping unreadable heartbeat file\"\n336\t                    );\n337\t                }\n338\t            }\n339\t        }\n340\t\n341\t        Ok(heartbeats)\n342\t    }\n343\t\n344\t    /// Check whether a heartbeat is stale (exceeded TTL).\n345\t    pub fn is_stale(heartbeat: &HeartbeatData, ttl: Duration) -> bool {\n346\t        let age = Utc::now()\n347\t            .signed_duration_since(heartbeat.last_heartbeat)\n348\t            .to_std()\n349\t            .unwrap_or(Duration::ZERO);\n350\t        age > ttl\n351\t    }\n352\t\n353\t    /// Check whether a process with the given PID is alive.\n354\t    ///\n355\t    /// Uses `kill(pid, 0)` semantics: sends signal 0 to check existence\n356\t    /// without actually delivering a signal. Returns true if the process\n357\t    /// exists (including EPERM — process exists but we can't signal it).\n358\t    pub fn check_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n359\t        if pid == 0 {\n360\t            return false;\n361\t        }\n362\t        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence; no signal is delivered.\n363\t        let rc = unsafe { libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t, 0) };\n364\t        if rc == 0 {\n365\t            return true;\n366\t        }\n367\t        // ESRCH means no such process. EPERM means the process exists but we\n368\t        // lack permission to send signals to it (process is alive).\n369\t        let errno = unsafe { *libc::__errno_location() };\n370\t        errno == libc::EPERM\n371\t    }\n372\t\n373\t    /// Detect peers with stale heartbeats.\n374\t    ///\n375\t    /// Returns a list of stale peers, excluding this worker.\n376\t    pub fn detect_stale_peers(&self) -> Result<Vec<StalePeer>> {\n377\t        let heartbeats = Self::read_all_heartbeats(&self.heartbeat_dir)?;\n378\t        let mut stale = Vec::new();\n379\t\n380\t        for hb in heartbeats {\n381\t            // Skip our own heartbeat.\n382\t            if hb.qualified_id == self.qualified_id {\n383\t                continue;\n384\t            }\n385\t\n386\t            if Self::is_stale(&hb, self.heartbeat_ttl) {\n387\t                let pid_alive = Self::check_pid_alive(hb.pid);\n388\t                let hb_file = hb.heartbeat_file.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n389\t                    self.heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", hb.qualified_id))\n390\t                });\n391\t                stale.push(StalePeer {\n392\t                    worker_id: hb.worker_id.clone(),\n393\t                    qualified_id: Some(hb.qualified_id.clone()),\n394\t                    pid: hb.pid,\n395\t                    pid_alive,\n396\t                    current_bead: hb.current_bead.clone(),\n397\t                    last_heartbeat: hb.last_heartbeat,\n398\t                    heartbeat_file: hb_file,\n399\t                });\n400\t            }\n401\t        }\n402\t\n403\t        Ok(stale)\n404\t    }\n405\t\n406\t    // ── Internal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n407\t\n408\t    /// Write a heartbeat file atomically (write temp, then rename).\n409\t    fn write_heartbeat(&self) -> Result<()> {\n410\t        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) = {\n411\t            let guard = self\n412\t                .shared_state\n413\t                .lock()\n414\t                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"shared state lock poisoned: {e}\"))?;\n415\t            (\n416\t                guard.state.clone(),\n417\t                guard.current_bead.clone(),\n418\t                guard.beads_processed,\n419\t                guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n420\t                guard.model.clone(),\n421\t            )\n422\t        };\n423\t\n424\t        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n425\t        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| self.workspace.clone());\n426\t\n427\t        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n428\t        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n429\t\n430\t        let data = HeartbeatData {\n431\t            worker_id: self.worker_id.clone(),\n432\t            qualified_id: self.qualified_id.clone(),\n433\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n434\t            state,\n435\t            current_bead,\n436\t            workspace: effective_workspace,\n437\t            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n438\t            started_at: self.started_at,\n439\t            beads_processed,\n440\t            session: self.worker_id.clone(),\n441\t            is_idle,\n442\t            current_task,\n443\t            model,\n444\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n445\t        };\n446\t\n447\t        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n448\t        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n449\t\n450\t        // Auto-create parent directory so that heartbeats self-recover if the\n451\t        // directory is deleted while a worker is running.\n452\t        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n453\t            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)\n454\t                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create heartbeat dir: {}\", parent.display()))?;\n455\t        }\n456\t\n457\t        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data).context(\"failed to serialize heartbeat\")?;\n458\t        std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes()).with_context(|| {\n459\t            format!(\n460\t                \"failed to write temp heartbeat file: {}\",\n461\t                tmp_path.display()\n462\t            )\n463\t        })?;\n464\t        std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path).with_context(|| {\n465\t            format!(\n466\t                \"failed to rename heartbeat file: {} -> {}\",\n467\t                tmp_path.display(),\n468\t                path.display()\n469\t            )\n470\t        })?;\n471\t\n472\t        Ok(())\n473\t    }\n474\t}\n475\t\n476\timpl Drop for HealthMonitor {\n477\t    fn drop(&mut self) {\n478\t        // Best-effort: signal the emitter and clean up the heartbeat file.\n479\t        self.stop();\n480\t    }\n481\t}\n482\t\n483\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n484\t// StalePeer\n485\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n486\t\n487\t/// A peer worker detected as having a stale heartbeat.\n488\t#[derive(Debug)]\n489\tpub struct StalePeer {\n490\t    pub worker_id: String,\n491\t    /// Fully-qualified identity of the peer.\n492\t    pub qualified_id: Option<String>,\n493\t    pub pid: u32,\n494\t    pub pid_alive: bool,\n495\t    pub current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n496\t    pub last_heartbeat: DateTime<Utc>,\n497\t    pub heartbeat_file: PathBuf,\n498\t}\n499\t\n500\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n501\t// Emitter loop (runs in a dedicated std::thread)\n502\t// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n503\t\n504\t/// Maximum sleep between heartbeat attempts when backing off on failure.\n505\tconst MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5 * 60);\n506\t\n507\t/// Sleep interval for interruptible sleep — check shutdown flag every 100ms.\n508\t///\n509\t/// This ensures the heartbeat emitter responds to shutdown signals within 100ms\n510\t/// instead of sleeping for the full heartbeat interval (default 60s). When the\n511\t/// worker is killed (e.g., by the capacity governor), this gives the atexit\n512\t/// handler a better chance to emit worker.stopped telemetry.\n513\tconst INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);\n514\t\n515\t/// Background emitter loop. Writes heartbeat at each interval.\n516\t///\n517\t/// Circuit breaker: after `max_consecutive_failures` consecutive write failures\n518\t/// the loop sets the shutdown flag and exits so the worker terminates instead of\n519\t/// spinning indefinitely.\n520\t///\n521\t/// Backoff: each consecutive failure doubles the inter-attempt sleep, capped at\n522\t/// [`MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF`].\n523\t///\n524\t/// Uses an interruptible sleep pattern to respond quickly to shutdown signals.\n525\t#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\n526\tfn emitter_loop(\n527\t    shared_state: Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>,\n528\t    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n529\t    heartbeat_dir: PathBuf,\n530\t    worker_id: String,\n531\t    qualified_id: String,\n532\t    workspace: PathBuf,\n533\t    started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n534\t    interval: Duration,\n535\t    max_consecutive_failures: u32,\n536\t) {\n537\t    // Ensure the heartbeat directory exists before entering the write loop so\n538\t    // that workers self-recover if ~/.needle/state/heartbeats/ is deleted.\n539\t    if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&heartbeat_dir) {\n540\t        tracing::error!(\n541\t            error = %e,\n542\t            dir = %heartbeat_dir.display(),\n543\t            \"failed to create heartbeat directory\"\n544\t        );\n545\t    }\n546\t\n547\t    let mut consecutive_failures: u32 = 0;\n548\t    let mut current_sleep = interval;\n549\t    let mut elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n550\t\n551\t    loop {\n552\t        // Interruptible sleep: check shutdown flag every 100ms instead of\n553\t        // sleeping for the full interval. This ensures the emitter responds\n554\t        // to shutdown signals quickly, giving the atexit handler a chance to\n555\t        // emit worker.stopped telemetry even if the process is killed.\n556\t        while elapsed < current_sleep {\n557\t            if shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n558\t                tracing::debug!(worker = %worker_id, \"heartbeat emitter shutting down\");\n559\t                return;\n560\t            }\n561\t            let sleep_dur = std::cmp::min(INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL, current_sleep - elapsed);\n562\t            std::thread::sleep(sleep_dur);\n563\t            elapsed += sleep_dur;\n564\t        }\n565\t        elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n566\t\n567\t        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) =\n568\t            match shared_state.lock() {\n569\t                Ok(guard) => (\n570\t                    guard.state.clone(),\n571\t                    guard.current_bead.clone(),\n572\t                    guard.beads_processed,\n573\t                    guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n574\t                    guard.model.clone(),\n575\t                ),\n576\t                Err(_) => {\n577\t                    // Mutex poisoned — the main thread panicked. Exit.\n578\t                    tracing::error!(\n579\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n580\t                        \"shared state mutex poisoned, heartbeat emitter exiting\"\n581\t                    );\n582\t                    return;\n583\t                }\n584\t            };\n585\t\n586\t        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n587\t        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| workspace.clone());\n588\t\n589\t        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n590\t        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n591\t\n592\t        let data = HeartbeatData {\n593\t            worker_id: worker_id.clone(),\n594\t            qualified_id: qualified_id.clone(),\n595\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n596\t            state,\n597\t            current_bead,\n598\t            workspace: effective_workspace,\n599\t            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n600\t            started_at,\n601\t            beads_processed,\n602\t            session: worker_id.clone(),\n603\t            is_idle,\n604\t            current_task,\n605\t            model,\n606\t            heartbeat_file: None,\n607\t        };\n608\t\n609\t        let path = heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", qualified_id));\n610\t        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n611\t\n612\t        let write_result: anyhow::Result<()> = (|| {\n613\t            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data)?;\n614\t            std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes())?;\n615\t            std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path)?;\n616\t            Ok(())\n617\t        })();\n618\t\n619\t        match write_result {\n620\t            Ok(()) => {\n621\t                consecutive_failures = 0;\n622\t                current_sleep = interval;\n623\t            }\n624\t            Err(e) => {\n625\t                consecutive_failures += 1;\n626\t                tracing::error!(\n627\t                    error = %e,\n628\t                    worker = %worker_id,\n629\t                    consecutive_failures,\n630\t                    max = max_consecutive_failures,\n631\t                    \"heartbeat write failed\"\n632\t                );\n633\t                if consecutive_failures >= max_consecutive_failures {\n634\t                    tracing::error!(\n635\t                        worker = %worker_id,\n636\t                        consecutive_failures,\n637\t                        \"heartbeat emitter circuit breaker triggered — worker will shut down\"\n638\t                    );\n639\t                    // Emit a final heartbeat event before shutting down so the\n640\t                    // telemetry log shows the circuit breaker was the cause.\n641\t                    let _ = std::fs::write(\n642\t                        heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}-circuit-breaker.txt\", qualified_id)),\n643\t                        format!(\n644\t                            \"Circuit breaker tripped after {} consecutive heartbeat write failures\\n\\\n645\t                             Worker: {}\\n\\\n646\t                             Qualified ID: {}\\n\\\n647\t                             Last error: {}\\n\\\n648\t                             Timestamp: {}\",\n649\t                            consecutive_failures,\n650\t                            worker_id,\n651\t                            qualified_id,\n652\t                            e,\n653\t                            Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n654\t                        ),\n655\t                    );\n656\t                    shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n657\t                    return;\n658\t                }\n659\t                // Exponential backoff to reduce log spam before the circuit breaker fires.\n660\t                current_sleep = current_sleep.saturating_mul(2).min(MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF);\n661\t            }\n662\t        }\n663\t    }\n664\t}\n665\t\n666\t//  let hb_dir = dir.path();\n775\t\n776\t        // Write two heartbeat files.\n777\t        let hb1 = HeartbeatData {\n778\t            worker_id: \"worker-a\".to_string(),\n779\t /// Verify that the circuit breaker fires after N consecutive write failures:\n976\t    /// the emitter must set the shutdown flag and return rather than looping forever.\n977\t    #[test]\n978\t    fn emitter_exits_after_consecutive_failures() {\n979\t        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n980\t\n981\t        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n982\t        let hb_dir = Health monitoring: heartbeats, stale detection, PID checking.\n//!\n//! Workers emit periodic heartbeats from a dedicated background thread.\n//! Peers read heartbeat files to detect crashed or stuck workers.\n//!\n//! The heartbeat emitter uses `std::thread::spawn` (not tokio) to keep it\n//! independent of the async runtime. The main worker updates shared state\n//! via `Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>`.\n//!\n//! Depends on: `config`, `types`.\n\nuse std::path::{Path, PathBuf};\nuse std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};\nuse std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};\nuse std::time::Duration;\n\nuse anyhow::{Context, Result};\nuse chrono::{DateTime, Utc};\nuse serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};\n\nuse crate::config::Config;\nuse crate::telemetry::Telemetry;\nuse crate::types::{BeadId, WorkerState};\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HeartbeatData — on-disk JSON structure\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Data written to the heartbeat JSON file on disk.\n///\n/// Path: `~/.needle/state/heartbeats/<qualified-id>.json`\n///\n/// This structure is compatible with cgov's Heartbeat format to ensure\n/// proper worker state detection and scaling decisions.\n#[derive(Debug, Clone, Serialize, Deserialize)]\npub struct HeartbeatData {\n    /// Bare NATO name (e.g., \"alpha\", \"foxtrot\").\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    /// Fully-qualified identity: `{adapter}-{worker_id}` (e.g., \"claude-code-glm-5-foxtrot\").\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub qualified_id: String,\n    pub pid: u32,\n    pub state: WorkerState,\n    pub current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n    pub workspace: PathBuf,\n    pub last_heartbeat: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub beads_processed: u64,\n    pub session: String,\n    /// Whether the worker is currently idle (no active task).\n    ///\n    /// A worker is considered idle when it's in EXHAUSTED state (all strands\n    /// returned NoWork) or when it has no current bead. This field is used by\n    /// cgov to determine which workers to scale down first.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub is_idle: bool,\n    /// Current task ID if any (cgov compatibility field).\n    ///\n    /// This maps the bead_id to cgov's expected current_task field.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub current_task: Option<String>,\n    /// Model being used (cgov compatibility field).\n    ///\n    /// Derived from the adapter configuration.\n    #[serde(default)]\n    pub model: String,\n    /// The filename that produced this heartbeat (set during read, not serialized).\n    #[serde(skip)]\n    pub heartbeat_file: Option<PathBuf>,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// SharedHeartbeatState — updated by worker, read by emitter\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Shared state between the main worker loop and the heartbeat emitter thread.\nstruct SharedHeartbeatState {\n    state: WorkerState,\n    current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n    beads_processed: u64,\n    /// The workspace of the current bead (updates dynamically during cross-workspace work).\n    current_workspace: Option<PathBuf>,\n    /// Model being used (from adapter configuration).\n    model: String,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// HealthMonitor\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Health monitor for a single worker.\n///\n/// Owns the background heartbeat emitter thread and provides reader utilities\n/// for peer heartbeat files.\npub struct HealthMonitor {\n    heartbeat_dir: PathBuf,\n    heartbeat_interval: Duration,\n    heartbeat_ttl: Duration,\n    /// Bare NATO name (e.g., \"alpha\", \"foxtrot\").\n    worker_id: String,\n    /// Fully-qualified identity: `{adapter_slug}-{worker_id}` (e.g., \"claude-code-glm-5-foxtrot\").\n    qualified_id: String,\n    workspace: PathBuf,\n    started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    shared_state: Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>,\n    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n    emitter_handle: Option<std::thread::JoinHandle<()>>,\n}\n\nimpl HealthMonitor {\n    /// Create a new health monitor.\n    ///\n    /// Does not start the emitter — call `start_emitter()` after construction.\n    ///\n    /// # Arguments\n    ///\n    /// * `config` - Worker configuration\n    /// * `worker_name` - Bare NATO name (e.g., \"alpha\", \"foxtrot\")\n    /// * `_telemetry` - Telemetry emitter (unused, kept for API compatibility)\n    /// * `shutdown` - Optional shared shutdown flag. If provided, the emitter's\n    ///   circuit breaker will set this flag to trigger graceful worker shutdown.\n    ///   If None, a private flag is created (test compatibility).\n    pub fn new(\n        config: Config,\n        worker_name: String,\n        _telemetry: Telemetry,\n        shutdown: Option<Arc<AtomicBool>>,\n    ) -> Self {\n        let heartbeat_dir = config.workspace.home.join(\"state\").join(\"heartbeats\");\n        let heartbeat_interval = Duration::from_secs(config.health.heartbeat_interval_secs);\n        let heartbeat_ttl = Duration::from_secs(config.health.heartbeat_ttl_secs);\n        let qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", config.agent.default, worker_name);\n\n        HealthMonitor {\n            heartbeat_dir,\n            heartbeat_interval,\n            heartbeat_ttl,\n            worker_id: worker_name,\n            qualified_id,\n            workspace: config.workspace.default.clone(),\n            started_at: Utc::now(),\n            shared_state: Arc::new(Mutex::new(SharedHeartbeatState {\n                state: WorkerState::Booting,\n                current_bead: None,\n                beads_processed: 0,\n                current_workspace: None,\n                model: config.agent.default.clone(),\n            })),\n            shutdown: shutdown.unwrap_or_else(|| Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false))),\n            emitter_handle: None,\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Start the background heartbeat emitter thread.\n    ///\n    /// The thread writes a heartbeat JSON file every `heartbeat_interval` until\n    /// `stop()` is called.\n    pub fn start_emitter(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n        // Ensure heartbeat directory exists.\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(&self.heartbeat_dir).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to create heartbeat directory: {}\",\n                self.heartbeat_dir.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        // Write the initial heartbeat immediately.\n        self.write_heartbeat()?;\n\n        let shared_state = self.shared_state.clone();\n        let shutdown = self.shutdown.clone();\n        let heartbeat_dir = self.heartbeat_dir.clone();\n        let worker_id = self.worker_id.clone();\n        let qualified_id = self.qualified_id.clone();\n        let workspace = self.workspace.clone();\n        let started_at = self.started_at;\n        let interval = self.heartbeat_interval;\n\n        let handle = std::thread::Builder::new()\n            .name(format!(\"heartbeat-{}\", self.worker_id))\n            .spawn(move || {\n                emitter_loop(\n                    shared_state,\n                    shutdown,\n                    heartbeat_dir,\n                    worker_id,\n                    qualified_id,\n                    workspace,\n                    started_at,\n                    interval,\n                    10,\n                );\n            })\n            .context(\"failed to spawn heartbeat emitter thread\")?;\n\n        self.emitter_handle = Some(handle);\n        tracing::info!(\n            worker = %self.worker_id,\n            interval_secs = self.heartbeat_interval.as_secs(),\n            \"heartbeat emitter started\"\n        );\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n\n    /// Update the worker state visible to the heartbeat emitter.\n    ///\n    /// Called by the worker on every state transition.\n    pub fn update_state(\n        &self,\n        state: &WorkerState,\n        current_bead: Option<&BeadId>,\n        workspace: Option<&Path>,\n    ) {\n        if let Ok(mut guard) = self.shared_state.lock() {\n            guard.state = state.clone();\n            guard.current_bead = current_bead.cloned();\n            guard.current_workspace = workspace.map(|p| p.to_path_buf());\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Update the beads_processed count visible to the heartbeat emitter.\n    pub fn update_beads_processed(&self, count: u64) {\n        if let Ok(mut guard) = self.shared_state.lock() {\n            guard.beads_processed = count;\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Stop the heartbeat emitter and remove this worker's heartbeat file.\n    ///\n    /// Called on graceful shutdown (STOPPED) and best-effort on ERRORED.\n    pub fn stop(&mut self) {\n        // Signal the emitter thread to exit.\n        self.shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n\n        // Join the emitter thread (with a timeout to avoid hanging).\n        if let Some(handle) = self.emitter_handle.take() {\n            // Give the thread up to 2x the interval to notice shutdown and exit.\n            let _ = handle.join();\n        }\n\n        // Remove the heartbeat file (best-effort).\n        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n        if path.exists() {\n            if let Err(e) = std::fs::remove_file(&path) {\n                tracing::warn!(\n                    error = %e,\n                    path = %path.display(),\n                    \"failed to remove heartbeat file on shutdown\"\n                );\n            } else {\n                tracing::debug!(path = %path.display(), \"heartbeat file removed\");\n            }\n        }\n    }\n\n    /// Path to this worker's heartbeat file.\n    ///\n    /// Keyed by fully-qualified identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`) to prevent\n    /// collisions when workers from different adapter pools share a NATO name.\n    pub fn heartbeat_path(&self) -> PathBuf {\n        self.heartbeat_dir\n            .join(format!(\"{}.json\", self.qualified_id))\n    }\n\n    /// The fully-qualified identity (`{adapter}-{worker_id}`).\n    pub fn qualified_id(&self) -> &str {\n        &self.qualified_id\n    }\n\n    /// Directory where heartbeat files are stored.\n    pub fn heartbeat_dir(&self) -> &Path {\n        &self.heartbeat_dir\n    }\n\n    /// The configured heartbeat TTL.\n    pub fn heartbeat_ttl(&self) -> Duration {\n        self.heartbeat_ttl\n    }\n\n    // ── Reader utilities (used by peer monitoring / Mend strand) ────────────\n\n    /// Read all heartbeat files in the given directory.\n    ///\n    /// Silently skips files that cannot be read or parsed (they may be\n    /// partially written or from a crashed worker).\n    pub fn read_all_heartbeats(dir: &Path) -> Result<Vec<HeartbeatData>> {\n        let mut heartbeats = Vec::new();\n\n        let entries = match std::fs::read_dir(dir) {\n            Ok(e) => e,\n            Err(e) if e.kind() == std::io::ErrorKind::NotFound => return Ok(heartbeats),\n            Err(e) => {\n                return Err(anyhow::anyhow!(\n                    \"failed to read heartbeat directory {}: {}\",\n                    dir.display(),\n                    e\n                ));\n            }\n        };\n\n        for entry in entries {\n            let entry = match entry {\n                Ok(e) => e,\n                Err(_) => continue,\n            };\n\n            let path = entry.path();\n            if path.extension().and_then(|e| e.to_str()) != Some(\"json\") {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            match std::fs::read_to_string(&path) {\n                Ok(content) => match serde_json::from_str::<HeartbeatData>(&content) {\n                    Ok(mut hb) => {\n                        // Backfill qualified_id for heartbeats written by older versions.\n                        if hb.qualified_id.is_empty() {\n                            hb.qualified_id = hb.worker_id.clone();\n                        }\n                        hb.heartbeat_file = Some(path.clone());\n                        heartbeats.push(hb)\n                    }\n                    Err(e) => {\n                        tracing::debug!(\n                            path = %path.display(),\n                            error = %e,\n                            \"skipping unparseable heartbeat file\"\n                        );\n                    }\n                },\n                Err(e) => {\n                    tracing::debug!(\n                        path = %path.display(),\n                        error = %e,\n                        \"skipping unreadable heartbeat file\"\n                    );\n                }\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(heartbeats)\n    }\n\n    /// Check whether a heartbeat is stale (exceeded TTL).\n    pub fn is_stale(heartbeat: &HeartbeatData, ttl: Duration) -> bool {\n        let age = Utc::now()\n            .signed_duration_since(heartbeat.last_heartbeat)\n            .to_std()\n            .unwrap_or(Duration::ZERO);\n        age > ttl\n    }\n\n    /// Check whether a process with the given PID is alive.\n    ///\n    /// Uses `kill(pid, 0)` semantics: sends signal 0 to check existence\n    /// without actually delivering a signal. Returns true if the process\n    /// exists (including EPERM — process exists but we can't signal it).\n    pub fn check_pid_alive(pid: u32) -> bool {\n        if pid == 0 {\n            return false;\n        }\n        // SAFETY: kill(pid, 0) only checks existence; no signal is delivered.\n        let rc = unsafe { libc::kill(pid as libc::pid_t, 0) };\n        if rc == 0 {\n            return true;\n        }\n        // ESRCH means no such process. EPERM means the process exists but we\n        // lack permission to send signals to it (process is alive).\n        let errno = unsafe { *libc::__errno_location() };\n        errno == libc::EPERM\n    }\n\n    /// Detect peers with stale heartbeats.\n    ///\n    /// Returns a list of stale peers, excluding this worker.\n    pub fn detect_stale_peers(&self) -> Result<Vec<StalePeer>> {\n        let heartbeats = Self::read_all_heartbeats(&self.heartbeat_dir)?;\n        let mut stale = Vec::new();\n\n        for hb in heartbeats {\n            // Skip our own heartbeat.\n            if hb.qualified_id == self.qualified_id {\n                continue;\n            }\n\n            if Self::is_stale(&hb, self.heartbeat_ttl) {\n                let pid_alive = Self::check_pid_alive(hb.pid);\n                let hb_file = hb.heartbeat_file.clone().unwrap_or_else(|| {\n                    self.heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", hb.qualified_id))\n                });\n                stale.push(StalePeer {\n                    worker_id: hb.worker_id.clone(),\n                    qualified_id: Some(hb.qualified_id.clone()),\n                    pid: hb.pid,\n                    pid_alive,\n                    current_bead: hb.current_bead.clone(),\n                    last_heartbeat: hb.last_heartbeat,\n                    heartbeat_file: hb_file,\n                });\n            }\n        }\n\n        Ok(stale)\n    }\n\n    // ── Internal ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n    /// Write a heartbeat file atomically (write temp, then rename).\n    fn write_heartbeat(&self) -> Result<()> {\n        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) = {\n            let guard = self\n                .shared_state\n                .lock()\n                .map_err(|e| anyhow::anyhow!(\"shared state lock poisoned: {e}\"))?;\n            (\n                guard.state.clone(),\n                guard.current_bead.clone(),\n                guard.beads_processed,\n                guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n                guard.model.clone(),\n            )\n        };\n\n        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| self.workspace.clone());\n\n        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n\n        let data = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: self.worker_id.clone(),\n            qualified_id: self.qualified_id.clone(),\n            pid: std::process::id(),\n            state,\n            current_bead,\n            workspace: effective_workspace,\n            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n            started_at: self.started_at,\n            beads_processed,\n            session: self.worker_id.clone(),\n            is_idle,\n            current_task,\n            model,\n            heartbeat_file: None,\n        };\n\n        let path = self.heartbeat_path();\n        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n\n        // Auto-create parent directory so that heartbeats self-recover if the\n        // directory is deleted while a worker is running.\n        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {\n            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)\n                .with_context(|| format!(\"failed to create heartbeat dir: {}\", parent.display()))?;\n        }\n\n        let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data).context(\"failed to serialize heartbeat\")?;\n        std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes()).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to write temp heartbeat file: {}\",\n                tmp_path.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n        std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path).with_context(|| {\n            format!(\n                \"failed to rename heartbeat file: {} -> {}\",\n                tmp_path.display(),\n                path.display()\n            )\n        })?;\n\n        Ok(())\n    }\n}\n\nimpl Drop for HealthMonitor {\n    fn drop(&mut self) {\n        // Best-effort: signal the emitter and clean up the heartbeat file.\n        self.stop();\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// StalePeer\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// A peer worker detected as having a stale heartbeat.\n#[derive(Debug)]\npub struct StalePeer {\n    pub worker_id: String,\n    /// Fully-qualified identity of the peer.\n    pub qualified_id: Option<String>,\n    pub pid: u32,\n    pub pid_alive: bool,\n    pub current_bead: Option<BeadId>,\n    pub last_heartbeat: DateTime<Utc>,\n    pub heartbeat_file: PathBuf,\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Emitter loop (runs in a dedicated std::thread)\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n/// Maximum sleep between heartbeat attempts when backing off on failure.\nconst MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5 * 60);\n\n/// Sleep interval for interruptible sleep — check shutdown flag every 100ms.\n///\n/// This ensures the heartbeat emitter responds to shutdown signals within 100ms\n/// instead of sleeping for the full heartbeat interval (default 60s). When the\n/// worker is killed (e.g., by the capacity governor), this gives the atexit\n/// handler a better chance to emit worker.stopped telemetry.\nconst INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL: Duration = Duration::from_millis(100);\n\n/// Background emitter loop. Writes heartbeat at each interval.\n///\n/// Circuit breaker: after `max_consecutive_failures` consecutive write failures\n/// the loop sets the shutdown flag and exits so the worker terminates instead of\n/// spinning indefinitely.\n///\n/// Backoff: each consecutive failure doubles the inter-attempt sleep, capped at\n/// [`MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF`].\n///\n/// Uses an interruptible sleep pattern to respond quickly to shutdown signals.\n#[allow(clippy::too_many_arguments)]\nfn emitter_loop(\n    shared_state: Arc<Mutex<SharedHeartbeatState>>,\n    shutdown: Arc<AtomicBool>,\n    heartbeat_dir: PathBuf,\n    worker_id: String,\n    qualified_id: String,\n    workspace: PathBuf,\n    started_at: DateTime<Utc>,\n    interval: Duration,\n    max_consecutive_failures: u32,\n) {\n    // Ensure the heartbeat directory exists before entering the write loop so\n    // that workers self-recover if ~/.needle/state/heartbeats/ is deleted.\n    if let Err(e) = std::fs::create_dir_all(&heartbeat_dir) {\n        tracing::error!(\n            error = %e,\n            dir = %heartbeat_dir.display(),\n            \"failed to create heartbeat directory\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    let mut consecutive_failures: u32 = 0;\n    let mut current_sleep = interval;\n    let mut elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n\n    loop {\n        // Interruptible sleep: check shutdown flag every 100ms instead of\n        // sleeping for the full interval. This ensures the emitter responds\n        // to shutdown signals quickly, giving the atexit handler a chance to\n        // emit worker.stopped telemetry even if the process is killed.\n        while elapsed < current_sleep {\n            if shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n                tracing::debug!(worker = %worker_id, \"heartbeat emitter shutting down\");\n                return;\n            }\n            let sleep_dur = std::cmp::min(INTERRUPTIBLE_SLEEP_INTERVAL, current_sleep - elapsed);\n            std::thread::sleep(sleep_dur);\n            elapsed += sleep_dur;\n        }\n        elapsed = Duration::ZERO;\n\n        let (state, current_bead, beads_processed, current_workspace, model) =\n            match shared_state.lock() {\n                Ok(guard) => (\n                    guard.state.clone(),\n                    guard.current_bead.clone(),\n                    guard.beads_processed,\n                    guard.current_workspace.clone(),\n                    guard.model.clone(),\n                ),\n                Err(_) => {\n                    // Mutex poisoned — the main thread panicked. Exit.\n                    tracing::error!(\n                        worker = %worker_id,\n                        \"shared state mutex poisoned, heartbeat emitter exiting\"\n                    );\n                    return;\n                }\n            };\n\n        // Use the current bead's workspace if set, otherwise fall back to home workspace.\n        let effective_workspace = current_workspace.unwrap_or_else(|| workspace.clone());\n\n        let is_idle = state == WorkerState::Exhausted || current_bead.is_none();\n        let current_task = current_bead.as_ref().map(|b| b.to_string());\n\n        let data = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: worker_id.clone(),\n            qualified_id: qualified_id.clone(),\n            pid: std::process::id(),\n            state,\n            current_bead,\n            workspace: effective_workspace,\n            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n            started_at,\n            beads_processed,\n            session: worker_id.clone(),\n            is_idle,\n            current_task,\n            model,\n            heartbeat_file: None,\n        };\n\n        let path = heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}.json\", qualified_id));\n        let tmp_path = path.with_extension(\"json.tmp\");\n\n        let write_result: anyhow::Result<()> = (|| {\n            let json = serde_json::to_string_pretty(&data)?;\n            std::fs::write(&tmp_path, json.as_bytes())?;\n            std::fs::rename(&tmp_path, &path)?;\n            Ok(())\n        })();\n\n        match write_result {\n            Ok(()) => {\n                consecutive_failures = 0;\n                current_sleep = interval;\n            }\n            Err(e) => {\n                consecutive_failures += 1;\n                tracing::error!(\n                    error = %e,\n                    worker = %worker_id,\n                    consecutive_failures,\n                    max = max_consecutive_failures,\n                    \"heartbeat write failed\"\n                );\n                if consecutive_failures >= max_consecutive_failures {\n                    tracing::error!(\n                        worker = %worker_id,\n                        consecutive_failures,\n                        \"heartbeat emitter circuit breaker triggered — worker will shut down\"\n                    );\n                    // Emit a final heartbeat event before shutting down so the\n                    // telemetry log shows the circuit breaker was the cause.\n                    let _ = std::fs::write(\n                        heartbeat_dir.join(format!(\"{}-circuit-breaker.txt\", qualified_id)),\n                        format!(\n                            \"Circuit breaker tripped after {} consecutive heartbeat write failures\\n\\\n                             Worker: {}\\n\\\n                             Qualified ID: {}\\n\\\n                             Last error: {}\\n\\\n                             Timestamp: {}\",\n                            consecutive_failures,\n                            worker_id,\n                            qualified_id,\n                            e,\n                            Utc::now().to_rfc3339()\n                        ),\n                    );\n                    shutdown.store(true, Ordering::SeqCst);\n                    return;\n                }\n                // Exponential backoff to reduce log spam before the circuit breaker fires.\n                current_sleep = current_sleep.saturating_mul(2).min(MAX_HEARTBEAT_BACKOFF);\n            }\n        }\n    }\n}\n\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n// Tests\n// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n\n#[cfg(test)]\nmod tests {\n    use super::*;\n    use std::time::Duration;\n\n    fn test_config(heartbeat_dir: &Path) -> Config {\n        let mut config = Config::default();\n        config.workspace.home = heartbeat_dir\n            .parent()\n            .unwrap()\n            .parent()\n            .unwrap()\n            .to_path_buf();\n        config.health.heartbeat_interval_secs = 1;\n        config.health.heartbeat_ttl_secs = 5;\n        config\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn heartbeat_file_written_on_start() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let hb_dir = dir.path().join(\"state\").join(\"heartbeats\");\n        let config = test_config(&hb_dir);\n        let mut monitor = HealthMonitor::new(\n            config,\n            \"test-worker\".to_string(),\n            Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string()),\n            None,\n        );\n\n        monitor.start_emitter().unwrap();\n\n        // The initial heartbeat is written synchronously in start_emitter().\n        let path = monitor.heartbeat_path();\n        assert!(path.exists(), \"heartbeat file should exist after start\");\n\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(&path).unwrap();\n        let data: HeartbeatData = serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(data.worker_id, \"test-worker\");\n        assert_eq!(data.pid, std::process::id());\n\n        monitor.stop();\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn heartbeat_updates_with_shared_state() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let hb_dir = dir.path().join(\"state\").join(\"heartbeats\");\n        let config = test_config(&hb_dir);\n        let mut monitor = HealthMonitor::new(\n            config,\n            \"state-test\".to_string(),\n            Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string()),\n            None,\n        );\n\n        monitor.start_emitter().unwrap();\n\n        // Update shared state.\n        monitor.update_state(\n            &WorkerState::Executing,\n            Some(&BeadId::from(\"needle-abc\")),\n            None,\n        );\n        monitor.update_beads_processed(5);\n\n        // Wait for the emitter to write a new heartbeat.\n        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(1500));\n\n        let content = std::fs::read_to_string(monitor.heartbeat_path()).unwrap();\n        let data: HeartbeatData = serde_json::from_str(&content).unwrap();\n        assert_eq!(data.state, WorkerState::Executing);\n        assert_eq!(data.current_bead, Some(BeadId::from(\"needle-abc\")));\n        assert_eq!(data.beads_processed, 5);\n\n        monitor.stop();\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn heartbeat_file_removed_on_stop() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n \"stop-test\".to_string(),\n            Telemetry::new(\"test\".to_string()),\n            None,\n        );\n\n        monitor.start_emitter().unwrap();\n        let path = monitor.heartbeat_path();\n        assert!(path.exists());\n\n        monitor.stop();\n        assert!(\n            !path.exists(),\n            \"heartbeat file should be removed after stop\"\n        );\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn read_all_heartbeats_reads_files() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let hb_dir = dir.path();\n\n        // Write two heartbeat files.\n        let hb1 = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: \"worker-a\".to_string(),\n            qualified_id: \"claude-worker-a\".to_string(),\n            pid: 1000,\n            state: WorkerState::Selecting,\n            current_bead: None,\n            workspace: PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n            started_at: Utc::now(),\n            beads_processed: 0,\n            session: \"worker-a\".to_string(),\n            is_idle: false,\n            current_task: None,\n            model: \"claude-sonnet-4\".to_string(),\n            heartbeat_file: None,\n        };\n        let hb2 = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: \"worker-b\".to_string(),\n            qualified_id: \"claude-worker-b\".to_string(),\n            pid: 2000,\n            state: WorkerState::Executing,\n            current_bead: Some(BeadId::from(\"nd-x\")),\n            workspace: PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n            started_at: Utc::now(),\n            beads_processed: 3,\n            session: \"worker-b\".to_string(),\n            is_idle: false,\n            current_task: Some(\"nd-x\".to_string()),\n            model: \"claude-sonnet-4\".to_string(),\n            heartbeat_file: None,\n        };\n\n        std::fs::write(\n            hb_dir.join(\"worker-a.json\"),\n            serde_json::to_string(&hb1).unwrap(),\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        std::fs::write(\n            hb_dir.join(\"worker-b.json\"),\n            serde_json::to_string(&hb2).unwrap(),\n        )\n        .unwrap();\n        // Non-JSON file should be skipped.\n        std::fs::write(hb_dir.join(\"README.txt\"), \"ignore me\").unwrap();\n\n        let heartbeats = HealthMonitor::read_all_heartbeats(Path::new(\"/nonexistent/dir\"));\n        assert!(result.is_ok());\n        assert!(result.unwrap().is_empty());\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn is_stale_detects_old_heartbeats() {\n        let mut hb = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: \"test\".to_string(),\n            qualified_id: \"claude-test\".to_string(),\n            pid: 1,\n            state: WorkerState::Selecting,\n            current_bead: None,\n            workspace: PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n            last_heartbeat: Utc::now(),\n            started_at: Utc::now(),\n            beads_processed: 0,\n            session: \"test\".to_string(),\n            is_idle: false,\n            current_task: None,\n            model: \"claude-sonnet-4\".to_string(),\n            heartbeat_file: None,\n        };\n\n        // Fresh heartbeat should not be stale.\n        assert!(!HealthMonitor::is_stale(&hb, Duration::from_secs(300)));\n\n        // Old heartbeat should be stale.\n        hb.last_heartbeat = Utc::now() - chrono::Duration::seconds(600);\n        assert!(HealthMonitor::is_stale(&hb, Duration::from_secs(300)));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn check_pid_alive_current_process() {\n        // Our own PID should be alive.\n        assert!(HealthMonitor::check_pid_alive(std::process::id()));\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn check_pid_alive_nonexistent() {\n        // PID 99999999 is almost certainly not running.\n        assert!(!HealthMonitor::check_pid_alive(99_999_999));\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn atomic_write_never_produces_partial() {\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let content\n                );\n            }\n            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));\n        }\n\n        monitor.stop();\n    }\n\n    #[test]\n    fn heartbeat_data_roundtrip() {\n        let data = HeartbeatData {\n            worker_id: \"test-rt\".to_string(),\n            qualified_id: \"claude-test-rt\".to_string(),\n            pid: 42,\n            state: failures:\n    /// the emitter must set the shutdown flag and return rather than looping forever.\n    #[test]\n    fn emitter_exits_after_consecutive_failures() {\n        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;\n\n        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().unwrap();\n        let hb_dir = dir.path().join(\"state\").join(\"heartbeats\");\n        std::fs::create_dir_all(&hb_dir).unwrap();\n\n        // Make the heartbeat directory read-only so every write attempt fails.\n        std::fs::set_permissions(&hb_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o555)).unwrap();\n\n        // Verify the directory is actually unwritable. When running as root\n        // (e.g., in CI containers), 0o555 doesn't block writes and the emitter\n        // loop would never hit its failure threshold, hanging the test forever.\n        let probe = hb_dir.join(\".write-probe\");\n        let unwritable = std::fs::write(&probe, b\"x\").is_err();\n        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&probe);\n        if !unwritable {\n            // Root bypasses permission checks — skip this test.\n            let _ = std::fs::set_permissions(&hb_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755));\n            return;\n        }\n\n        let shared_state = Arc::new(Mutex::new(SharedHeartbeatState {\n            state: WorkerState::Selecting,\n            current_bead: None,\n            beads_processed: 0,\n            current_workspace: None,\n            model: \"claude-sonnet-4\".to_string(),\n        }));\n        let shutdown = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(false));\n\n        let shutdown_clone = shutdown.clone();\n        let shared_state_clone = shared_state.clone();\n        let hb_dir_clone = hb_dir.clone();\n\n        // Use a tiny interval and a low failure threshold so the test completes quickly.\n        let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {\n            emitter_loop(\n                shared_state_clone,\n                shutdown_clone,\n                hb_dir_clone,\n                \"cb-test\".to_string(),\n                \"claude-cb-test\".to_string(),\n                PathBuf::from(\"/tmp\"),\n                Utc::now(),\n                Duration::from_millis(1),\n                3, // trip after 3 consecutive failures\n            );\n        });\n\n        handle.join().expect(\"emitter thread panicked\");\n\n        // The circuit breaker must have set the shutdown flag.\n        assert!(\n            shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst),\n            \"shutdown flag must be set after circuit breaker trips\"\n        );\n\n        // Restore permissions so the tempdir can be cleaned up.\n        let _ = std::fs::set_permissions(&hb_dir, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755));\n    }\n\n    #[tokio::test]\n    async fn heartbeat_path_uses_qualified_id_not_bare_worker_id() {\n        let dir the worker is stuck in HANDLING state even if\n494\t    /// the Tokio runtime becomes wedged. If HANDLING state exceeds the\n495\t    /// timeout, the watchdog sets the `watchdog_triggered` flag, which\n496\t    /// the main worker loop checks to force recovery.\n497\t    fn start_watchdog_thread(&mut self) {\n498\t        let watchdog_triggered = self.watchdog_triggered.clone();\n499\t        let handling_state_entered_at_ptr =\n500\t            &self.handling_state_entered_at as *const Option<Instant> as usize;\n501\t\n502\t        let handle = std::thread::spawn(move || {\n503\t            loop {\n504\t                std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_secs(5));\n505\t                if watchdog_triggered.load(Ordering::Relaxed) {\n506\t                    // Watchdog has already triggered, exit the thread.\n507\t                    break;\n508\t                }\n509\t                // Check if we've been in HANDLING state for too long.\n510\t                // We read the timestamp from the Worker struct via the pointer.\n511\t                // SAFETY: The Worker struct outlives the watchdog thread because\n512\t                // the thread is joined when the Worker is dropped.\n513\t                let entered_at = unsafe {\n514\t                    let ptr = handling_state_entered_at_ptr as *const Option<Instant>;\n515\t                    (*ptr).as_ref().copied()\n516\t                };\n517\t\n518\t                if let Some(entry_time) = entered_at {\n519\t                    let elapsed = entry_time.elapsed().as_secs();\n520\t                    if elapsed >= HANDLING_WATCHDOG_TIMEOUT_SECS {\n521\t                        tracing::error!(\n522\t                            elapsed_secs = elapsed,\n523\t                            \"HANDLING state watchdog triggered - forcing recovery\"\n524\t                        );\n525\t                        watchdog_triggered.store(true, Ordering::Release);\n526\t                        break;\n527\t                    }\n528\t                }\n529\t            }\n530\t        });\n531\t\n532\t        self.watchdog_handle = Some(handle);\n533\t    }\n534\t\n535\t    /// Run the worker loop until exhausted, stopped, or errored.\n536\t    ///\n537\t    /// The main loop is a match on `self.state`. Every state has a handler\n538\t    /// that performs its actions and sets `self.state` to the next state.\n539\t    ///\n540\t    /// Guarantees that the telemetry BufWriter is flushed before returning,\n541\t    /// even when the inner state machine exits early via `?`.\n542\t    pub async fn run(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n543\t        // Start the telemetry writer now that we are inside the tokio runtime.\n544\t        self.telemetry.start();\n545\t\n546\t        // IMMEDIATE boot event — must be the first thing emitted after telemetry starts.\n547\t        // This ensures we get a trace even if subsequent init steps block indefinitely.\n548\t        // Skip if already emitted externally (e.g., from CLI layer for early boot diagnostics).\n549\t        if !self.booting_emitted {\n550\t            self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::WorkerBooting {\n551\t                worker_name: self.worker_name.clone(),\n552\t                version: env!(\"CARGO_PKG_VERSION\").to_string(),\n553\t            })?;\n554\t            // Force-flush to disk before boot() — if init blocks, we still have a trace.\n555\t            self.telemetry\n556\t                .force_flush_async(std::time::Duration::from_secs(5))\n557\t                .await?;\n558\t        }\n559\t\n560\t        let result = self.run_inner().await;\n561\t\n562\t        // Safety-net flush: shutdown() is idempotent. Normal terminal paths\n563\t        // (stop, handle_exhausted, Errored) already call it; this catches\n564\t        // any early-exit via `?` (boot failure, state handler panic, etc.)\n565\t        // so the BufWriter is always flushed before the tokio Runtime drops.\n566\t        self.telemetry.shutdown().await;\n567\t\n568\t        result\n569\t    }\n570\t\n571\t    /// Inner state machine — called only from [`run()`](Self::run).\n572\t    ///\n573\t    /// May return early via `?` without calling `telemetry.shutdown()`;\n574\t    /// `run()` handles the safety-net flush.\n575\t    async fn run_inner(&mut self) -> Result<WorkerState> {\n576\t        // Boot: validate config and initialize.\n577\t        self.boot()?;\n578\t\n579\t        // Start the watchdog thread that monitors %self.telemetry.session_id(),\n592\t            needle.agent = %self.config.agent.default,\n593\t            needle.model = %self.config.agent.default, // Will be updated when adapter is resolved\n594\t            needle.workspace = %self.config.workspace.default.display(),\n595\t        );\n596\t        let _enter = session_span.enter();\n597\t\n598\t        loop {\n599\t            // Check for shutdown signal between states.\n600\t            if self.shutdown.load(Ordering::SeqCst) {\n601\t                // Retrieve and clear the last received signal for logging.\n602\t                #[cfg(unix)]\n603\t                let signal_name = {\n604\t                    let sig = LAST_SIGNAL.swap(0, AtomicOrdering::SeqCst);\n605\t                    if sig == 0 {\n606\t                        None\n607\t                    } else {\n608\t                        Some(match sig {\n609\t                            1 => \"SIGHUP\",\n610\t                            2 => format!(\"signal received ({name})\")\n621\t                } else {\n622\t                    \"signal received\".to_string()\n623\t                };\n624\t\n625\t                match self.state {\n626\t                    // If we're in the middle of processing a bead, release it\n627\t                    // before stopping. This ensures the bead is returned to\n628\t                    // the open state and can be claimed by another worker.\n629\t                    WorkerState::Building\n630\t                    | WorkerState::Dispatching\n631\t                    | WorkerState::Executing\n632\t                    | WorkerState::Handling => {\n633\t                        // Release any claimed bead before                        return self.stop(\"signal received during boot\").await;\n651\t                    }\n652\t                }\n653\t            }\n654\t\n655\t            // Check for watchdog                }\n683\t                // Clear the watchdog trigger and force transition to LOGGING.\n684\t                self.watchdog_triggered.store(false, Ordering::Release);\n685\t            match self.state {\n691\t                WorkerState::Selecting => self.do_select().await?,\n692\t                WorkerState::Claiming => self.do_claim().await?,\n693\t                WorkerState::Retrying => self.do_retry().await?,\n694\t                WorkerState::Building => self.do_build().await?,\n695\t                WorkerState::Dispatching =>    // ── Boot ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────\n748\t\n749\t    /// Validate configuration and initialize the worker.\n750\t    ///\n751\t    /// Each step is instrumented with `init.step.started`/`init.step.completed`\n752\t    /// events so that hangs are visible in the telemetry log. Boot duration is\n753\t    /// capped at 60 seconds — if exceeded, the worker self-aborts with a\n754\t    /// `worker.boot.timeout` event and exits with a non-zero code.\n755\t    fn boot(&mut self) -> Result<()> {\n756\t        self.boot_time = Some(Instant::now());\n757\t        const BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS: u64 = 60;\n758\t\n759\t        // Step: Config validation\n760\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepStarted {\n761\t            step: \"config_validation\".to_string(),\n762\t        })?;\n763\t        let step_start = Instant::now();\n764\t        let errors =         .collect::<Vec<_>>()\n770\t                .join(\"; \");\n771\t            bail!(\"config validation failed: {msg}\");\n772\t        }\n773\t })?;\n777\t\n778\t        // Check boot timeout before each step\n779\t        self.check_boot_timeout(BOOT_TIMEOUT_SECS)?;\n780\t\n781\t        // Step: Registry registration\n782\t qualified_id = format!(\"{}-{}\", self.config.agent.default, self.worker_name);\n787\t        let entry = WorkerEntry {\n788\t            id: qualified_id.clone(),\n789\t            pid: std::process::id(),\n790\t            workspace: self.config.workspace.default.clone(),\n791\t            agent: self.config.agent.default.clone(),\n792\t            model: None,\n793\t = %e, \"failed to start heartbeat emitter\");\n815\t        }\n816\t        self.telemetry.emit(EventKind::InitStepCompleted {\n817\t            step: \"heartbeat_emitter\".to_string(),\n818\t
